Overall, there are other places in Gulf Shores that you could visit and have a better dinner.

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 1cd4bee1-e9da-4e6f-a152-4fa677fb9cd8
entity_slug: hammered-crab
reviewer_name: Elizabeth Miller
rating: 1
body: Was definitely not the experience we were hoping for. 4 of the 5 people in our party were not impressed with their food. Just average. Nothing to write home about.

One of the other reviews stated that the smell of seafood was overwhelming and that is definitely correct. The hostess was unfriendly from the start. We were asked to wait for over 5 minutes for a table when the restaurant was mostly empty. Very odd.

The server was fine. She was very polite and attentive.

As I stated earlier, the food was just average. 2 people had Gumbo and it looked nasty (like it had been sitting for a while).  Coleslaw had very little flavor. Ribs were dry and spicy. Served with very little sauce on the side. French dip was edible but not good. Chicken fingers were the only thing that was decent. The 2 people who ordered gumbo both ended up getting sick and felt horrible for over 24 hours.

Overall, there are other places in Gulf Shores that you could visit and have a better dinner.
